vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-600-/// # Mapping memory
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-601-///
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-602-/// Maps the queue associated device's resource.
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-603-///
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs:604:/// Mapping resources could have been quite straightforward if OpenCL wouldn't allow for so called
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-605-/// non blocking maps. Non blocking maps shall return a valid pointer to the mapped region
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-606-/// immediately, but should not synchronize data (in case of shadow buffers) until after the map
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-607-/// event is reached in the queue. This makes it not possible to simply use pipe_transfers as those
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-608-/// can't be explicitly synced by the frontend.
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-609-///
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-610-/// In order to have a compliant implementation of the mapping API we have to consider the following
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-611-/// cases:
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-612-///   1. Mapping a cl_mem object with CL_MEM_USE_HOST_PTR: We simply return the host_ptr.
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-613-///      Synchronization of shadowed host ptrs are done in `sync_shadow` on demand.
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs:614:///   2. Mapping linear resources on UMA systems: We simply create the pipe_transfer with
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-615-///      `PIPE_MAP_DIRECTLY` and `PIPE_MAP_UNSYNCHRONIZED` and return the attached pointer.
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-616-///   3. On non UMA systems or when 2. fails (e.g. due to the resource being tiled) we
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-617-///      - create a shadow pipe_resource with `PIPE_USAGE_STAGING`,
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-618-///        `PIPE_RESOURCE_FLAG_MAP_PERSISTENT` and `PIPE_RESOURCE_FLAG_MAP_COHERENT`
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-619-///      - create a pipe_transfer with `PIPE_MAP_COHERENT`, `PIPE_MAP_PERSISTENT` and
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-620-///        `PIPE_MAP_UNSYNCHRONIZED`
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-621-///      - sync the shadow buffer like a host_ptr shadow buffer in 1.
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-622-///
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-623-/// Taking this approach we guarentee that we only copy when actually needed while making sure the
vendor-src/mesa-26.0.3/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-624-/// content behind the returned pointer is valid until unmapped.

599-
600-/// # Mapping memory
601-///
602-/// Maps the queue associated device's resource.
603-///
604-/// Mapping resources could have been quite straightforward if OpenCL wouldn't allow for so called
605-/// non blocking maps. Non blocking maps shall return a valid pointer to the mapped region
606-/// immediately, but should not synchronize data (in case of shadow buffers) until after the map
607-/// event is reached in the queue. This makes it not possible to simply use pipe_transfers as those
608-/// can't be explicitly synced by the frontend.
609-///
610-/// In order to have a compliant implementation of the mapping API we have to consider the following
611-/// cases:
612:///   1. Mapping a cl_mem object with CL_MEM_USE_HOST_PTR: We simply return the host_ptr.
613-///      Synchronization of shadowed host ptrs are done in `sync_shadow` on demand.
614-///   2. Mapping linear resources on UMA systems: We simply create the pipe_transfer with
615-///      `PIPE_MAP_DIRECTLY` and `PIPE_MAP_UNSYNCHRONIZED` and return the attached pointer.
616-///   3. On non UMA systems or when 2. fails (e.g. due to the resource being tiled) we
617-///      - create a shadow pipe_resource with `PIPE_USAGE_STAGING`,
618-///        `PIPE_RESOURCE_FLAG_MAP_PERSISTENT` and `PIPE_RESOURCE_FLAG_MAP_COHERENT`
619-///      - create a pipe_transfer with `PIPE_MAP_COHERENT`, `PIPE_MAP_PERSISTENT` and
620-///        `PIPE_MAP_UNSYNCHRONIZED`
621:///      - sync the shadow buffer like a host_ptr shadow buffer in 1.
622-///
623-/// Taking this approach we guarentee that we only copy when actually needed while making sure the
624-/// content behind the returned pointer is valid until unmapped.
625-pub struct MemBase {
626-    pub base: CLObjectBase<CL_INVALID_MEM_OBJECT>,
627-    pub context: Arc<Context>,
628-    pub mem_type: cl_mem_object_type,
629-    pub flags: cl_mem_flags,
630-    pub size: usize,
631-    pub props: Properties<cl_mem_properties>,
632-    pub cbs: Mutex<Vec<MemCB>>,
633-    pub gl_obj: Option<GLObject>,
634-    alloc: Allocation,
635-}

641-}

vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-601-/// # Mapping memory
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-602-///
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-603-/// Maps the queue associated device's resource.
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-604-///
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs:605:/// Mapping resources could have been quite straightforward if OpenCL wouldn't allow for so called
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-606-/// non blocking maps. Non blocking maps shall return a valid pointer to the mapped region
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-607-/// immediately, but should not synchronize data (in case of shadow buffers) until after the map
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-608-/// event is reached in the queue. This makes it not possible to simply use pipe_transfers as those
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-609-/// can't be explicitly synced by the frontend.
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-610-///
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-611-/// In order to have a compliant implementation of the mapping API we have to consider the following
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-612-/// cases:
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-613-///   1. Mapping a cl_mem object with CL_MEM_USE_HOST_PTR: We simply return the host_ptr.
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-614-///      Synchronization of shadowed host ptrs are done in `sync_shadow` on demand.
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs:615:///   2. Mapping linear resources on UMA systems: We simply create the pipe_transfer with
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-616-///      `PIPE_MAP_DIRECTLY` and `PIPE_MAP_UNSYNCHRONIZED` and return the attached pointer.
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-617-///   3. On non UMA systems or when 2. fails (e.g. due to the resource being tiled) we
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-618-///      - create a shadow pipe_resource with `PIPE_USAGE_STAGING`,
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-619-///        `PIPE_RESOURCE_FLAG_MAP_PERSISTENT` and `PIPE_RESOURCE_FLAG_MAP_COHERENT`
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-620-///      - create a pipe_transfer with `PIPE_MAP_COHERENT`, `PIPE_MAP_PERSISTENT` and
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-621-///        `PIPE_MAP_UNSYNCHRONIZED`
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-622-///      - sync the shadow buffer like a host_ptr shadow buffer in 1.
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-623-///
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-624-/// Taking this approach we guarentee that we only copy when actually needed while making sure the
vendor-src/mesa-26.2.0/src/gallium/frontends/rusticl/core/memory.rs-625-/// content behind the returned pointer is valid until unmapped.

601-/// # Mapping memory
602-///
603-/// Maps the queue associated device's resource.
604-///
605-/// Mapping resources could have been quite straightforward if OpenCL wouldn't allow for so called
606-/// non blocking maps. Non blocking maps shall return a valid pointer to the mapped region
607-/// immediately, but should not synchronize data (in case of shadow buffers) until after the map
608-/// event is reached in the queue. This makes it not possible to simply use pipe_transfers as those
609-/// can't be explicitly synced by the frontend.
610-///
611-/// In order to have a compliant implementation of the mapping API we have to consider the following
612-/// cases:
613:///   1. Mapping a cl_mem object with CL_MEM_USE_HOST_PTR: We simply return the host_ptr.
614-///      Synchronization of shadowed host ptrs are done in `sync_shadow` on demand.
615-///   2. Mapping linear resources on UMA systems: We simply create the pipe_transfer with
616-///      `PIPE_MAP_DIRECTLY` and `PIPE_MAP_UNSYNCHRONIZED` and return the attached pointer.
617-///   3. On non UMA systems or when 2. fails (e.g. due to the resource being tiled) we
618-///      - create a shadow pipe_resource with `PIPE_USAGE_STAGING`,
619-///        `PIPE_RESOURCE_FLAG_MAP_PERSISTENT` and `PIPE_RESOURCE_FLAG_MAP_COHERENT`
620-///      - create a pipe_transfer with `PIPE_MAP_COHERENT`, `PIPE_MAP_PERSISTENT` and
621-///        `PIPE_MAP_UNSYNCHRONIZED`
622:///      - sync the shadow buffer like a host_ptr shadow buffer in 1.
623-///
624-/// Taking this approach we guarentee that we only copy when actually needed while making sure the
625-/// content behind the returned pointer is valid until unmapped.
626-pub struct MemBase {
627-    pub base: CLObjectBase<CL_INVALID_MEM_OBJECT>,
628-    pub context: Arc<Context>,
629-    pub mem_type: cl_mem_object_type,
630-    pub flags: cl_mem_flags,
631-    pub size: usize,
632-    pub props: Properties<cl_mem_properties>,
633-    pub cbs: Mutex<Vec<MemCB>>,
634-    pub gl_obj: Option<GLObject>,
635-    alloc: Allocation,
636-}
